A failed agent's is dropped:\n               nothing it did is evidence. The parsed-result block below reads\n               directives and drafts, which no agent produces. */\n            if (isAgent && (toolStatus === 'success' || toolStatus === 'incomplete')) {\n              for (const p of agentProvenance(resultStr)) {\n                if (collectedProvenance.length >= PROVENANCE_CAP) break;\n                collectedProvenance.push(p as ProvenanceRecord);\n              }\n            }\n            if (toolStatus === 'success' && !isAgent) {\n              try {\n                const parsed = JSON.parse(resultStr);\n                // Accumulate any provenance envelope this evidence tool emitted, for\n                // durable persistence to the lineage trail in post-processing.\n                if (Array.isArray(parsed?.provenance)) {\n                  for (const p of parsed.provenance) {\n                    if (collectedProvenance.length >= PROVENANCE_CAP) break;\n                    if (p && typeof p === 'object') collectedProvenance.push(p as ProvenanceRecord);\n                  }\n                }\n                // A validated navigation target AnA resolved this turn. Collected\n                // here, offered as a chip by post-processing; a refused target\n                // (unknown id, missing param) yields null and never becomes one.\n                const directive = directiveFromToolResult(toolUse.name, resultStr);\n                if (directive) {\n                  // Past the turn's budget the move is NOT applied — say so to\n                  // the model instead of the handler's \"being applied now\", and\n                  // hold it for the front of the chip list.\n                  if (driveState.enabled && driveNavigationsApplied >= driveBudget.navigations) {\n                    amendForModel(\n                      toolUse.id,\n                      parsed,\n                      'navigation',\n                      unappliedNavigation.length < MAX_NAVIGATION_ACTIONS\n                    );\n                    unappliedNavigation.push(directive);\n                  } else {\n                    collectedNavigation.push(directive);\n                  }\n                  // Live Drive: the person opted in and is entitled, so the\n                  // directive is ALSO emitted now for immediate application —\n                  // budgeted per mode, audited, and re-validated client-side\n                  // against the same shared registry before the screen moves.\n                  if (driveState.enabled && driveNavigationsApplied < driveBudget.navigations) {\n                    driveNavigationsApplied += 1;\n                    res.write(\n                      `data: ${JSON.stringify(\n                        buildDriveNavigationEvent(directive, round, toolUse.id)\n                      )}\\n\\n`\n                    );\n                    if (driveAcks)\n                      awaitingMoves.set(toolUse.id, `opening the ${directive.label} screen`);\n                    auditDriveNavigation(\n                      {\n                        organizationId: orgId,\n                        userId: userId ?? null,\n                        threadId,\n                        runId,\n                        round,\n                        directive,\n                        driveMode: driveState.mode,\n                      },\n                      entry => auditService.logAction(entry)\n                    );\n                  }\n                }\n                // A validated surface action AnA resolved this turn — the same\n                // carrier contract as navigation: chip via post-processing,\n                // applied live under Drive within the mode's action budget,\n                // audited per applied operation, client re-validates and only\n                // performs through a handler the mounted surface registered.\n                const actionDirective = surfaceActionFromToolResult(toolUse.name, resultStr);\n                if (actionDirective) {\n                  if (driveState.enabled && driveActionsApplied >= driveBudget.actions) {\n                    amendForModel(\n                      toolUse.id,\n                      parsed,\n                      'action',\n                      unappliedSurfaceActions.length < MAX_NAVIGATION_ACTIONS\n                    );\n                    unappliedSurfaceActions.push(actionDirective);\n                  } else {\n                    collectedSurfaceActions.push(actionDirective);\n                  }\n                  if (driveState.enabled && driveActionsApplied < driveBudget.actions) {\n                    driveActionsApplied += 1;\n                    res.write(\n                      `data: ${JSON.stringify(\n                        buildDriveActionEvent(actionDirective, round, toolUse.id)\n                      )}\\n\\n`\n                    );\n                    if (driveAcks) {\n                      awaitingMoves.set(\n                        toolUse.id,\n                        `\"${actionDirective.label}\" on the ${actionDirective.surfaceId} screen`\n                      );\n                    }\n                    auditDriveAction(\n                      {\n                        organizationId: orgId,\n                        userId: userId ?? null,\n                        threadId,\n                        runId,\n                        round,\n                        directive: actionDirective,\n                        driveMode: driveState.mode,\n                      },\n                      entry => auditService.logAction(entry)\n                    );\n                  }\n                }\n                const demoStart = demoStartFromToolResult(toolUse.name, resultStr);\n                if (demoStart) collectedDemoStarts.push(demoStart);\n                // A demonstration asked for in plain words (\"give me the sales\n                // demo\") arrives as an ordinary driving turn. Once the script\n                // is fetched, the turn IS a demonstration: promote it so the\n                // tour gets the demo budgets, prompt and round ceiling, and tell\n                // the client so its caps (and the next turns) follow.\n                if (\n                  toolUse.name === 'start_product_demo' &&\n                  parsed?.status === 'demo_ready' &&\n                  parsed?.driven === true &&\n                  driveState.enabled &&\n                  driveState.mode !== 'demo'\n                ) {\n                  driveState = { ...driveState, mode: 'demo' };\n                  driveBudget = driveBudgetFor('demo');\n                  // Marked `promoted`. Unmarked, this second drive_state was\n                  // indistinguishable from the turn-start one, and the client\n                  // read it as a fresh enable — re-arming a drive the person\n                  // had taken over or switched off moments earlier. It is a\n                  // mode change for a drive already running, and says so. The\n                  // turn-start event above never carries the flag.\n                  res.write(\n                    `data: ${JSON.stringify({\n                      ...buildDriveStateEvent(driveState),\n                      promoted: true,\n                    })}\\n\\n`\n                  );\n                  pendingOperatorTurns.push({\n                    role: 'system',\n                    inlineSystem: true,\n                    foldLabel: 'Operator instruction',\n                    content: buildLiveDrivePromptBlock('demo').trim(),\n                  });\n                }\n                if (parsed?.status === 'intelligence_question' && parsed.question) {\n                  res.write(\n                    `data: ${JSON.stringify({\n                      type: 'intelligence_question',\n                      question: parsed.question,\n                      flowState: parsed.flowState,\n                      sessionId: parsed.session_id ?? null,\n                    })}\\n\\n`\n                  );\n                }\n                if (parsed?.status === 'intelligence_flow_complete' && parsed.completion) {\n                  res.write(\n                    `data: ${JSON.stringify({\n                      type: 'intelligence_flow_complete',\n                      completion: parsed.completion,\n                      flowState: parsed.flowState,\n                      sessionId: parsed.session_id ?? null,\n                    })}\\n\\n`\n                  );\n                }\n                // War Game report — forward to client as a dedicated SSE event\n                if (parsed?.war_game_report) {\n                  res.write(\n                    `data: ${JSON.stringify({\n                      type: 'war_game_report',\n                      report: parsed.war_game_report,\n                    })}\\n\\n`\n                  );\n                }\n                // Reporting Canvas — a governed report render or a best-practices\n                // suggestion set from the reporting tools. Forwarded verbatim so the\n                // client canvas renders it (report → ReportView; suggestions → chips).\n                if (parsed?.report_canvas) {\n                  res.write(\n                    `data: ${JSON.stringify({\n                      type: 'report_canvas',\n                      canvas: parsed.report_canvas,\n                      source: toolUse.name,\n                    })}\\n\\n`\n                  );\n                }\n                if (\n                  parsed &&\n                  parsed.status === 'generated' &&\n                  typeof parsed.content === 'string' &&\n                  parsed.content.length > 0\n                ) {\n                  const draftTitle: string = parsed.title || 'Generated document';\n                  /* An authoring document (draft_authoring_document): the draft\n                     already lives in the editor's store under this id, in this\n                     program. The event carries both so the client renders the\n                     document canvas over that id instead of an artifact card,\n                     and post-processing does not write a second copy. */\n                  const authoringDocId: string | undefined =\n                    typeof parsed.authoringDocId === 'string' && parsed.authoringDocId\n                      ? parsed.authoringDocId\n                      : undefined;\n                  const authoringProgramId: string | undefined =\n                    authoringDocId && typeof parsed.programId === 'string' && parsed.programId\n                      ? parsed.programId\n                      : undefined;\n                  // Record for durable version-history persistence in post-processing.\n                  collectedDrafts.push({\n                    title: draftTitle,\n                    content: parsed.content,\n                    documentType:\n                      typeof parsed.documentType === 'string' ? parsed.documentType : undefined,\n                    reasonForChange:\n                      typeof parsed.reasonForChange === 'string'\n                        ? parsed.reasonForChange\n                        : undefined,\n                    ...(authoringDocId ? { authoringDocId, programId: authoringProgramId } : {}),\n                  });\n                  res.write(\n                    `data: ${JSON.stringify({\n                      type: 'artifact_draft',\n                      title: draftTitle,\n                      content: parsed.content,\n                      documentType: parsed.documentType,\n                      source: toolUse.name,\n                      ...(authoringDocId ? { authoringDocId, programId: authoringProgramId } : {}),\n                    })}\\n\\n`\n                  );\n                }\n              } catch {\n                // Non-JSON tool result — nothing to surface as a draft.\n              }\n            }\n          }\n\n          // Budget the whole round's results before they reach the model, so a\n          // many-tool round can't bloat every later round's context (deep loops\n          // carry all prior results forward). Small rounds pass through under the\n          // classic per-result caps, byte-identical to before. A read goes whole\n          // or not at all, and its receipt is written only if it went whole.\n          const staged = budgetToolResultsForModel(entries, { wholeOrNothing: readReceipts, strictTotal: true });\n          const budgeted = await guardReadResultsForHandoff(entries, staged, readReceipts, { signal: runSignal });\n          const sourceReadIds = [...new Set([...readReceipts.keys(), ...entries.filter(e => e.name === 'read_project_document').map(e => e.tool_use_id)])];\n          await settleReadReceipts(entries, budgeted, readReceipts, undefined, {\n            signal: runSignal, onSourceRefused: () => sourceReadIds.forEach(id => withdrawSourceReadResult(budgeted, id, { signal: runSignal })),\n          });\n          // Late source refusal can grow bodies after allocation. Do not trim\n          // after receipt settlement or claim earlier outcomes were rolled back.\n          assertToolResultBodyBudget(budgeted);\n          // What the model will read of each result, where the budget or the\n          // drive-budget amendment changed it from what the tool returned.\n          turnRecorder?.setSentToModel(budgeted);\n          // Ground against what the MODEL saw, not the raw results. If the\n          // grounding round verified the answer against fuller text than the model\n          // was fed, a claim sitting in the truncated-away middle would be marked\n          // \"grounded\" though the model never read it — a false pass in the one\n          // direction that lets a fabrication through. The corpus therefore gets\n          // exactly the budgeted strings the model gets, each with the call's\n          // status, input and the generations its handler made.\n          const outcomes = new Map(ran.map(r => [r.toolUse.id, r]));\n          for (const b of budgeted) {\n            const r = outcomes.get(b.tool_use_id);\n            if (r?.toolUse.name === RUN_AGENT_TOOL) {\n              /* An agent is evidence only through its tools' results, as the\n                 child read them — never its report, never the checks' echo of\n                 the text the parent gave it, never its notes (brief D19). */\n              if (r.toolStatus === 'success' || r.toolStatus === 'incomplete') {\n                toolEvidenceCorpus.push(...agentEvidenceEntries(r.resultStr));\n              }\n              continue;\n            }\n            const entry = r\n              ? toolEvidence(r.toolUse.name, {\n                  status: r.toolStatus,\n                  input: r.toolUse.input,\n                  content: b.content,\n                  generated: r.generated,\n                })\n              : null;\n            if (entry) toolEvidenceCorpus.push(entry);\n          }\n          // Failure guidance for the next model turn (cleared after use): the\n          // steps that failed, then the steps that returned nothing usable — a\n          // search with no hits, a service that could not be reached, a tool\n          // that needs input it was not given (tool-outcome.ts, TP-RL-4).\n          const stagedById = new Map(budgeted.map(entry => [entry.tool_use_id, entry.content]));\n          pendingAdaptationNote = [\n            buildAdaptationNote(roundFailures, calls.length),\n            buildShortfallNote(\n              ran.map(r => ({ label: describeToolPlan([r.toolUse])[0].label, status: r.toolStatus, heldBack: r.heldBack,\n                result: stagedById.get(r.toolUse.id) ?? '' })),\n              calls.length,\n            ),\n          ]\n            .filter(Boolean)\n            .join('\\n');\n          /* The round is done and the loop is about to hand these results back to\n           * the model. That is a genuinely different activity from running the\n           * tools, and it is the moment AnA decides whether she has enough or\n           * goes back for more — so it gets its own line rather than leaving the\n           * previous \"Running N steps…\" standing over work that has finished.\n           *\n           * This is also the longest silent window in a turn: the model may take\n           * many seconds to compose from a full round of results, and until this\n           * existed the user watched settled rows with no sign anything was still\n           * happening. */\n          res.write(\n            `data: ${JSON.stringify({\n              type: 'status',\n              phase: 'reading_results',\n              message: 'Reading the results…',\n            })}\\n\\n`\n          );\n          return budgeted;\n        };\n\n        // Call the model with the latest tool results, streaming its narration. On\n        // the terminal round includeTools is false to force a grounded answer.\n        const loopMessages: GatewayMessage[] = [...messages];\n        /* Stage one round's turns onto loopMessages: what the model said last,\n           the tool results it now has, and any operator steer waiting.\n\n           Separated from callModel below because it is bookkeeping over three\n           closure variables and callModel is where the round's MODEL decisions\n           live; reading them interleaved made both harder to follow, and the\n           mix pushed callModel past the complexity limit. */\n        const stageRound = (results: ToolResultEntry[], priorText: string, round: number): void => {\n          // Replan control results bypass executeTools; all staged rounds fit.\n          assertToolResultBodyBudget(results);\n          const stagedFrom = loopMessages.length;\n          loopMessages.push({\n            role: 'assistant',\n            content: assistantTurnContent(priorText, results),\n          });\n          // Preserve accepted body bytes after corpus/recorder admission.\n          // The adaptation note (when a tool failed\n          // last round) rides the same user turn so the model course-corrects\n          // instead of retrying the identical call.\n          const adaptationSuffix = pendingAdaptationNote ? `\\n\\n${pendingAdaptationNote}` : '';\n          pendingAdaptationNote = '';\n          loopMessages.push({\n            role: 'user',\n            content:\n              results\n                .map(\n                  tr =>\n                    `[Tool Result for ${tr.name} (${tr.tool_use_id})]:\\n${frameToolOutput(\n                      tr.content\n                    )}`\n                )\n                .join('\\n\\n') + adaptationSuffix,\n          });\n          // Steers ride AFTER the tool results, as operator turns. Order\n          // matters twice over: the gateway requires an inline system turn to\n          // follow a user turn, and a redirect read after the evidence is a\n          // redirect the model applies to this round rather than one it has\n          // already reasoned past.\n          if (pendingOperatorTurns.length > 0) {\n            loopMessages.push(...pendingOperatorTurns);\n            pendingOperatorTurns = [];\n          }\n          // Everything staged here is the next model call's new input.\n          turnRecorder?.addRoundInput(round, loopMessages.slice(stagedFrom));\n        };\n\n        /** Decision 3 (A): a round that offers tools asks for her notes as her words (gateway: progress-updates.ts). */\n        const notesBetweenTools = (includeTools: boolean): boolean => includeTools && streamTools.length > 0;\n\n        /** What a follow-up round sends, for its record (MC-RL-8): the closing round is told to call no tool. */\n        const roundSent = (thinking: ReturnType<typeof followUpThinking>, includeTools: boolean): CallSent =>\n          callSent({ apiEffort, thinking, tools: streamTools, toolChoice: includeTools ? null : 'none' });\n\n        const callModel = async (\n          results: ToolResultEntry[],\n          priorText: string,\n          round: number,\n          includeTools: boolean\n        ): Promise<ModelTurn> => {\n          if (runSignal?.aborted) return { text: '', toolCalls: [] };\n          await settleMoves(round);\n          if (runSignal?.aborted) return { text: '', toolCalls: [] };\n          stageRound(results, priorText, round);\n\n          // Model tiering (S3) — opt-in via ANA_LOOP_TIERING=on, default OFF so\n          // production behavior is byte-identical until deliberately enabled and\n          // validated. When on, intermediate follow-up rounds (round >= 2 that\n          // still carry tools) run on the latency-optimized tier so routine\n          // tool-result summarization stops paying top-tier latency. It NEVER\n          // downgrades a user-pinned model (resolvedOverride) and NEVER the forced\n          // final grounded answer (includeTools === false) — that stays on the\n          // resolved strategy, so the truthfulness/quality of the answer the user\n          // reads is unchanged.\n          const roundStrategy =\n            process.env.ANA_LOOP_TIERING === 'on' && !resolvedOverride && includeTools && round >= 2\n              ? 'latency_optimized'\n              : selectedStrategy;\n\n          let roundText = '';\n          // What this round sends, kept for its record (MC-RL-8).\n          const roundThinking = followUpThinking();\n          if (runSignal?.aborted) return { text: '', toolCalls: [] };\n          const roundResponse = await gw.route({\n            taskType: routingPlan.taskType,\n            // Every agentic round is bound to the same tenant as the first.\n            organizationId: orgId ?? undefined,\n            riskTier: routingPlan.riskTier,\n            runId: runId || undefined,\n            messages: loopMessages,\n            maxTokens: streamMaxTokens,\n            temperature: routingPlan.temperature,\n            strategy: roundStrategy,\n            // Keep the same explicit model (user pin or cost tier) across the\n            // agentic follow-up rounds so the whole turn stays on one tier.\n            ...(resolvedOverride\n              ? { provider: resolvedOverride.provider, model: resolvedOverride.model }\n              : tieredModel\n              ? { provider: tieredModel.provider, model: tieredModel.model }\n              : {}),\n            promptCache: { enabled: true, type: 'ephemeral' },\n            signal: runSignal,\n            // Pinned per TURN, not per round: changing effort mid-conversation\n            // invalidates the messages cache, and the follow-up rounds are the\n            // same piece of work as the first.\n            apiEffort,\n            // The turn's thinking config, as the first call's (MC-RL-6). These\n            // are the calls that read the tool results and write the answer:\n            // without it they did not reason on the legacy surface, and on the\n            // adaptive one their reasoning was never shown or kept. Tool turns\n            // travel as prose, so no thinking block needs replaying.\n            thinking: roundThinking,\n            // The tools array stays on the request for EVERY round, including\n            // the terminal one. Withdrawing it is what the terminal round used\n            // to do, and it cost the whole prompt cache once per turn:\n            // Anthropic's cache prefix renders tools -> system -> messages, and\n            // a tool-definition change (add, remove or reorder) is the one\n            // change that preserves NO cache tier. Dropping the array on the\n            // last round therefore rebuilt tools, system AND messages, every\n            // turn, at exactly the point the conversation was longest.\n            //\n            // `tool_choice: 'none'` gets the same behaviour — a grounded text\n            // answer with no further tool calls — while changing only a\n            // parameter that preserves the tools and system caches. Same\n            // outcome, one cache tier instead of none.\n            ...(streamTools.length > 0 ? { tools: streamTools } : {}),\n            ...(includeTools ? {} : { toolChoice: 'none' as const }),\n            // Decision 3 (A): a tool round has her notes as her words; the closing round keeps the summary.\n            notesBetweenTools: notesBetweenTools(includeTools),\n            stream: true,\n            onStream: (chunk: string, metadata?: any) => {\n              if (runHandle?.cancelSignal.aborted) return;\n              if (metadata?.type === 'thinking') {\n                const thinkingChunk: string = metadata?.thinkingContent || '';\n                if (thinkingChunk) {\n                  fullThinking += thinkingChunk;\n                  res.write(\n                    `data: ${JSON.stringify({ type: 'thinking', content: thinkingChunk })}\\n\\n`\n                  );\n                }\n                return;\n              }\n              // A round's words start their own paragraph. A model ends a note\n              // without a line break, and the next round's first words ran\n              // straight on from it: \"…on screen.Stop 2: …\".\n              const shown = roundText ? chunk : asParagraph(fullContent, chunk);\n              roundText += chunk;\n              fullContent += shown;\n              turnRecorder?.appendStreamed(shown);\n              res.write(`data: ${JSON.stringify({ type: 'text', content: shown })}\\n\\n`);\n            },\n            callerModule: 'ana-ri-stream-followup',\n          });\n          if (!roundText && roundResponse.content) {\n            roundText = roundResponse.content;\n            const appended = (fullContent ? '\\n\\n' : '') + roundText;\n            fullContent += appended;\n            recordStreamed(appended);\n            res.write(`data: ${JSON.stringify({ type: 'text', content: roundText })}\\n\\n`);\n          }\n          recordCacheUsage(roundResponse);\n          lastFinishReason = roundResponse.finishReason;\n          // Her words before the next round's steps, when this call also called tools (a note, S4).\n          timeline.noteFromCall(roundText, roundResponse);\n          emitServerToolSteps(roundResponse, round);\n          recordServerToolEvidence(roundResponse);\n          lastServedModel = servedModelOf(roundResponse);\n          commandRounds.push(...commandRoundOf(roundText, lastServedModel));\n          recordServed(round, lastServedModel, roundSent(roundThinking, includeTools));\n          const nextUses = (roundResponse as AnaGatewayResponse).toolUses;\n          return { text: roundText, toolCalls: (nextUses ?? []).map(toToolCall) };\n        };\n\n        // Round-boundary human control. Consulted by the loop before each\n        // round: hold while paused, splice queued steers into the next model\n        // turn, and abort on cancel.\n        //\n        // Status is read from the ROW, never from a process-local copy — the\n        // control may have been accepted by a different instance. The abort\n        // signal is the one exception and is not a cached status: it is the\n        // abort itself, and it is read per streamed chunk, so it has to be\n        // synchronous.\n        //\n        // The control EVENTS are not recorded here. They are written to the row\n        // by the control endpoint at the moment of acceptance, so a crash of\n        // this process cannot lose a human decision; what happens below is only\n        // telling the client what the server did.\n\n        /* Splice drained queue entries into the next model turn, and return the\n           steers among them for the caller to announce once it knows the run\n           was not cancelled (see the `interjected` note in the checkpoint). */\n        const spliceQueued = (entries: RunQueueEntry[]): string[] => {\n          const steers: string[] = [];\n          for (const queued of entries) {\n            // A landing only settles its move (settleMoves); nothing to read.\n            if (queued.kind === 'move_landed') continue;\n            if (queued.kind === 'screen_report') {\n              // What the APP saw on the person's screen — a move that did not\n              // land. Framed as an observation, and never announced as\n              // `interjected`: the client renders that as \"You steered AnA:\",\n              // and the person steered nothing.\n              const report = buildScreenReportTurn(queued.text);\n              if (report) pendingOperatorTurns.push(report);\n              continue;\n            }\n            const framed = buildSteerMessage(queued.text);\n            if (framed) {\n              pendingOperatorTurns.push({\n                role: 'system',\n                inlineSystem: true,\n                // Scanned, not exempt. It is the operator's channel, but the\n                // words are still typed by a human into a text box, and\n                // `origin: 'app'` is reserved for content our own code\n                // authored verbatim (see GatewayMessage.origin).\n                origin: 'external',\n                content: framed,\n              });\n            }\n            steers.push(queued.text);\n          }\n          return steers;\n        };\n\n        /* Before she writes the round that follows her moves, wait for the\n           screen to settle every one of them — landed, or refused with the\n           screen's own reason — bounded by MOVE_SETTLE_MAX_MS.\n\n           The model call used to follow the tool results at once. A move takes\n           the screen a moment (a navigation waits for its screen, an operation\n           for its data), so the report of one that failed arrived while she\n           was already writing, was read by no round, and she told the person\n           she had searched a vault that showed nothing, or opened a document\n           that did not open. order and frames without a policy are the ones it had here). */\n        const manualDemoExempt = driveState.enabled && driveState.mode === 'demo';\n        const checkpoint =            cancelled: () => runHandle!.cancelSignal.aborted,\n                  heartbeat: (round: number) => {\n                    void runHandle!.heartbeat(round);\n                  },\n                  // Steers and screen reports: the drain is atomic and covers\n                  // both, so neither can be applied twice.\n                  drainSteers: async () => spliceQueued(await consumeInterjections(getPool(), runId)),\n                  holdForPerson: () => holdForPerson(getPool(), runId),\n                  status: () => readStatus(getPool(), runId),\n                }\n              : null,\n          // Latched as the turn began: a demonstration the person started\n          // runs through, but one the model starts mid-turn (start_product_demo\n          // promotes });\n          },\n        });\n\n        // Kept for endRun: until 2026-09-26 the result was discarded and every\n        // run was recorded as ending for want of tools, including one stopped\n        // at its round ceiling or by the thrash guard (D6, plan WS3).\n        const loopResult = await runAgenticToolLoop(\n          { text: fullContent, toolCalls: streamToolUses.map(toToolCall) },\n          { executeTools, callModel, checkpoint },\n          // Effort-scaled agentic depth: Thorough can chase a multi-tool\n          // investigation all the way down; Balanced clears the old flat cap Demo mode raises (never lowers) the base: a narrated tour\n            // spends roughly one round per stop, so a full script must not be\n            // cut off at the effort ceiling Keeps the `done` event on the critical path latency-free.\n\n      // Telemetry attached to the `done` event. Phase timings let ops spot\n      // regressions in orchestration / context assembly / generation; cache\n      // stats confirm prompt caching is actually hitting; memory diagnostics\n      // show degraded layers without scraping logs.\n      const streamMemoryDiag = (memoryResult as any)?.diagnostics || null;\n      const streamTelemetry = {\n        phases: {\n          orchestrationMs: streamOrchestrationMs,\n          contextMs: streamContextMs,\n          gatewayMs: streamGatewayMs,\n          firstTokenMs: streamFirstTokenMs,\n        },\n        cache:\n          (gwResponse as any)?.cacheHit !== undefined\n            ? // Fix B: Enforce thread ownership — if thread_id was supplied, verify org match\n    if (requestedThreadId && numericOrgId) {\n      try {\n        const ownerCheck = await pool.query(\n await pool.query(\n          `INSERT INTO ai_threads (id, organization_id, project_id, created_by)\n           VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4) ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING`,\n          [threadId, numericOrgId,  }\n\n    const previousMessages = await getThreadMessages(threadId);\n\n    // ── STEP 2b: SUBMISSION-CHAT AUTO-FLIP ──────────────────────────────\n    // If the user supplied an artifactId in submissionChatArtifactId.length > 0 &&\n      (submissionChatExplicit ||\n        isPostSectionGenerationTurn(previousMessages));\n\n    if (shouldRunSubmissionChat) {\n      try {\n        const sub organizationUuid: orgUuid,\n          userId: numericUserId || null,\n        });\n\n        // Persist user + assistant turn to legacy chat_messages so the thread\n        // remains continuous when the next message flips back to normal mode.\n        await saveMessage(threadId, 'user', message, sub.model);\n        await saveMessage(\n          threadId,\n          'assistant',\n          sub.answer,\n          sub.model,\n          sub.usage.totalTokens\n        );\n\n        return res.json({\n          answer: sub.answer,\n          response: sub.answer,\n          thread_id: sub.usage.completionTokens,\n            total_tokens: sub.usage.totalTokens,\n          },\n          mode: 'submission-chat',\n          citations: sub.citations,\n          submissionChat: {\n conversation: sub.conversation,\n          },\n        });\n      } catch (err: any) {\n        // Fall through to the normal chat path on failure — the user still\n        // gets an answer, just without the cross-dossier scope.\n        console.warn(\n          '[AnA] submission-chat auto-flip failed, falling back to normal chat:',\n          err?.message\n        );\n      }\n    }\n\n    // ── STEP 3: PERSIST USER MESSAGE (provenance chain) ─────────────────\n    if (numericOrgId) {\n      try {\n        await pool.query(\n          `INSERT INTO ai_messages (thread_id, role, content) VALUES ($1, 'user', $2)`,\n          [threadId, message]\n        );\n        // Update thread activity timestamp for sort ordering\n        await pool\n          .query(`UPDATE ai_threads SET updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = $1`, [threadId])\n          .catch(err =>\n            console.warn('[AnA] thread updated_at touch failed (non-blocking):', err?.message)\n          );\n      } catch (e: any) {\n        if (e?.code !== '42P01') console.warn('[AnA] ai_messages insert failed:', e.message);\n      }\n    }\n\n    // ── STEP 3a: AUTO-GENERATE THREAD TITLE FROM FIRST MESSAGE ──────────\n    if (previousMessages.length === 0 && message) {\n      try {\n        // Generate a concise title from the first message (no AI call needed)\n        const rawTitle = message.replace(/^\\/\\w+\\s*/, '').trim(); // Strip slash commands\n        const title =\n          rawTitle.length > 60\n            ? rawTitle.slice(0, 57).replace(/\\s+\\S*$/, '') + '...'\n            : rawTitle || 'New // Non-blocking — title generation failure doesn't break chat\n        if (e?.code !== '42P01') console.warn('[AnA] Thread title update failed:', e.message);\n      }\n    }\n\n    const String(project_id).replace(/^proj_/, '')\n      : undefined;\n    // The integer project this turn's project names, resolved ONCE (PF-10\n    // S10b): an integer as itself, a program UUID through its anchor row,\n    // anything else as none. Each step below used to parseInt on its own, and\n    // parseInt('7abb1c22-…') is 7, a valid, wrong project of the same\n    // organization (services/c2c/project-ref.ts).\n    const turnProjectId =    // 'unavailable' when the search could not run (P1-54): told to the model and\n    // returned in retrievalMeta, never rendered as \"no sources found\".\n    let retrievalStatus: RetrievalStatus = 'searched';\n    let confidence: number | null = null;\n    let retrievalRunId: string | null = null;\n    let snapshotHashSha256: string | null = null;\n    const chunkRows: Array<{ id: string; rank: number; atomId: string; score: number }> = [];\n    try {\n      const embeddingService = getEmbeddingService(pool);\n      // RETRIEVAL_THRESHOLD is a floor on semantic similarity, and the one\n      // recorded in ai_retrieval_runs below; it used to go in as the ranking weight.\n      const searchResults = await embeddingService.searchHybrid(message, {\n        limit: RETRIEVAL_TOP_K,\n        organizationUuid: orgUuid,\n        projectId: normalizedProjectId,\n        minSemanticScore: RETRIEVAL_THRESHOLD,\n      });\n      sources = searchResults.map(r => ({\n        id: r.id,\n        title: r.title,\n        content: r.content.length > 500 ? r.content.substring(0, 500) + '…' : r.content,\n        score: r.score,\n      }));\n      if (sources.length > 0) {\n        confidence = Math.min(1, sources.reduce((sum, s) => sum + s.score, 0) / sources.length);\n      }\n\n      // Persist retrieval run + chunks (provenance chain)\n      if (numericOrgId) {\n        try {\n          const queryHash = sha256(message);\n          // Fix C: Snapshot hash includes sourceType + sourceRefId, sorted by rank\n          const snapshotData = sources.map((s, i) => ({\n            rank: i + 1,\n            sourceType: 'atom' as const,\n            sourceRefId: s.id,\n            score: s.score,\n          }));\n          snapshotHashSha256 = sha256(stableStringify(snapshotData));\n\n          const rrResult = await pool.query(\n            `INSERT INTO ai_retrieval_runs\n               (organization_id, project_id, user_id, scope, embedding_model,\n                query_text,    [\n              numericOrgId,\n              project_id || null,\n              numericUserId,\n              'org', // every search runs under the session's tenant key; there is no 'global' one\n              message,\n              queryHash,\n              snapshotHashSha256,\n              RETRIEVAL_TOP_K,\n              RETRIEVAL_THRESHOLD,\n              sources.length,\n            ]\n          );\n          retrievalRunId = rrResult.rows[0].id;\n\n          // AnA fix F9: batch all retrieval chunks into one multi-row INSERT\n          // (replaces a sequential N+1 loop — RETRIEVAL_TOP_K queries became 1).\n          if (sources.length > 0) {\n            const valuesSql: string[] = [];\n            const params: unknown[] = [];\n            const paramsPerRow = 7;\n            for (let i = 0; i < sources.length; i++) {\n              const s = sources[i];\n              const o = i * paramsPerRow;\n              valuesSql.push(\n                `($${o + 1}, $${o + 2}, 'atom', $${o + 3}, $${o + 4}, $${o + 5}, $${o + 6}, $${o + 7})`\n              );\n              params.push(\n                retrievalRunId,\n                i + 1,\n                s.id,\n                s.title,\n                sha256(s.content),\n                s.content.substring(0, 500),\n                s.score\n              );\n            }\n            const crResult = await pool.query(\n              `INSERT INTO ai_retrieval_chunks\n                 (retrieval_run_id, rank, source_type, atom_id, title,\n                  excerpt_hash_sha256, excerpt_preview, score)\n               VALUES ${valuesSql.join(', ')}\n               RETURNING id, rank, atom_id, score`,\n              params\n            );\n            for (const row of crResult.rows) {\n              chunkRows.push({\n                id: row.id,\n                rank: row.rank,\n                atomId: row.atom_id,\n                score: Number(row.score),\n              });\n            }\n            chunkRows.sort((a, b) => a.rank - b.rank);\n          }\n        } catch (e: any) {\n          if (e?.code !== '42P01') console.warn('[AnA] Retrieval persist failed:', e.message);\n        }\n      }\n    } catch (srcErr: any) {\n      // Non-fatal — chat still works, without grounded evidence, and says so.\n      retrievalStatus = 'unavailable';\n      console.warn('[AnA] Source retrieval failed:', srcErr.message);\n    }\n\n    // ── STEP 5: BUILD EVIDENCE-GROUNDED PROMPT ─────────────────────────\n    // If we have retrieved evidence, inject it into the system prompt so\n    // the model can ground its answer and cite by [SRC-n] reference.\n    let evidenceBlock = '';\n    let memoryAtomCount = 0;\n    let memoryBlockChars = 0;\n    let memoryDiagnostics: MemoryAssemblyDiagnostics | null = null;\n    evidenceBlock = evidencePromptBlock(sources, retrievalStatus);\n\n    let assistantMessage: string;\n    let model: string;\n    let provider = '';\n    // The model call whose reply carries the turn's ana-action blocks: its\n    // proposals are sealed with it (D5, MC-RL-4; ana-ri/proposal-seal.ts).\n    let servingModel: { provider: string; model: string; requestId: string | null } | null = null;\n    let usage = { prompt_tokens: 0, completion_tokens: 0, total_tokens: 0 };\n    let latencyMs: number | null = null;\n    // Hoisted so the response serializer (after the try/catch) can read it.\n    // The catch block returns 503, so reaching the response site implies\n    // the assignment inside the try succeeded — hence non-null at use.\n    let orchestratorResult: OrchestratorOutput | null = null;\n    // Validated navigation directives the agentic loop's navigate_to produced\n    // this turn. Hoisted for the same reason: collected inside the try (the\n    // loop's onToolExecution), offered as chips by the action step after it.\n    // This caller used to DROP them silently — AnA resolved a target, told the\n    // user she could take them there, and no chip ever reached this client.\n    const collectedNavigation: NavigationDirective[] = [];\n    // Surface actions ride the same carrier: act_on_screen results become\n    // offer-chips here too (this route never applies anything live).\n    const collectedSurfaceActions: SurfaceActionDirective[] = [];\n    const collectedDemoStarts: DemoStartDirective[] = [];\n    // The platform-command proposals the loop's execute_platform_command came\n    // back with. This route cannot hold the turn and ask, as the stream does;\n    // until P0-12's residual (2026-10-01) the proposal reached only the model,\n    // and the person had nothing to confirm. Returned as executedCommands.\n    const loopProposals: CommandResult[] = [];\n    // The turn's retained record (services/ana/turn-record-loop.ts): the tool\n    // calls as the loop reports them, filed when the turn answers or fails.\n    const loopCalls = loopToolCollector();\n    let loopMessages: Array<{ role: string; content: unknown }> = [];\n    const fileTurn = (outcome: 'answered' | 'failed', response?: unknown, error?: unknown): Promise<TurnRecordStatus> =>\n      recordLoopTurn(\n        getPool(),\n        {\n          orgId: numericOrgId,\n          userId: numericUserId,\n          surface: 'api:chat/send-message',\n          projectId: project_id ?? null,\n          threadId,\n          typed: typeof message === 'string' ? message : String(message ?? Configure ANTHROPIC_API_KEY or OPENAI_API_KEY.',\n        code: 'AI_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE',\n      });\n    }\n\n    try {\n      // Build conversation history for orchestrator continuity analysis\n      const conversationHistory = previousMessages\n        .filter((m: any) => m.role === 'user' || m.role === 'assistant')\n        .map((m: any) => ({ role: m.role as 'user' | 'assistant', content: m.content }));\n\n      // Pre-fetch decision context for AnA explanation grounding (non-blocking)\n      let decisionContext: any[] = [];\n      if (project_id) {\n        try {\n          const { decisionLifecycleService } =\n            await import('../../services/decision-lifecycle-service.js');\n          // Scoped to the caller's tenant: this context is fed to the\n          // orchestrator as grounding, so an unscoped read would not just\n          // leak another organization's decisions, it would let the model\n          // quote them back in an answer. numericOrgId is resolved above and\n          // used throughout this handler; the decision read was the one that\n          // did not pass it.\n          decisionContext = numericOrgId\n            ? {}) as Record<string, any>;\n      const orchestratorProjectContext = project_id\n        ? {\n            productName: clientCtx.activeProject || clientCtx.productName,\n            therapeuticArea: clientCtx.therapeuticArea,\n            submissionType: clientCtx.productType || clientCtx.submissionType,\n            targetAgency: clientCtx.targetAgency,\n            phase: clientCtx.phase,\n          }\n        : undefined;\n      const orchestratorDocumentContext =\n        clientCtx.artifactId || clientCtx.sectionCode || clientCtx.module\n          ? {\n              documentType: clientCtx.documentType || clientCtx.artifactType,\n              section: clientCtx.sectionCode,\n              module: clientCtx.module || clientCtx.moduleCode,\n            }\n          : undefined;\n\n      orchestratorResult = orchestrate({\n        message,\n        conversationHistory,\n        projectContext: orchestratorProjectContext,\n        documentContext: orchestratorDocumentContext,\n        userRole: clientCtx.userRole,\n        authoringContext: project_id\n          ? {\n              projectId: String(project_id),\n              sectionCode: clientCtx.sectionCode,\n              moduleCode: clientCtx.module || clientCtx.moduleCode,\n              artifactStatus: clientCtx.artifactStatus,\n              _decisionContext: decisionContext,\n            }\n          : undefined,\n      });\n\n      console.log(\n        `[AnA] Orchestrator: intent=${orchestratorResult.detectedIntent.lens}, ` +\n          `submission=${orchestratorResult.detectedSubmissionType || 'none'}, ` +\n          `deficiency=${orchestratorResult.orchestrationMeta.deficiencyContextInjected}`\n      );\n\n      // Use the orchestrator's enriched system prompt instead of the basic one\n      const basePrompt = system_prompt || orchestratorResult.systemPrompt;\n\n      // AnA fix F6: fetch intelligence prefix and persistent-memory context\n      // in parallel — they're independent and were previously awaited\n      // sequentially, costing ~100–200 ms per chat. The\n      // mode itself is surfaced separately by GET /projects/:id/knowledge.\n      let projectKnowledgeCorpusBlock = '';\n      if (INCONTEXT_INJECTION_ENABLED && turnProjectId !== null && numericOrgId) {\n        const pidNum = turnProjectId;\n        {\n          try {\n            const modeState = await getProjectRetrievalMode(pidNum, numericOrgId);\n            if (modeState.mode === 'in_context') {\n              projectKnowledgeCorpusBlock = await assembleProjectKnowledgeCorpus(\n                pidNum,\n                numericOrgId\n              );\n            }\n          } catch {\n            /* non-fatal — fall back to retrieval-only */\n          }\n        }\n      }\n\n      // AnA fix F2: always prepend a CONTEXT SNAPSHOT block so AnA can see\n      // exactly what context is loaded right now — including explicit\n      // \"NOT LOADED\" / \"NONE\" markers when something is missing. The Context\n      // Clarity Protocol in the persona forbids inferring what isn't here.\n      const snapshotProductName = clientCtx.activeProject || clientCtx.productName;\n      const snapshotSubmission = clientCtx.productType || clientCtx.submissionType;\n      const snapshotArtifactTitle = clientCtx.artifactTitle;\n      const snapshotSection = clientCtx.sectionCode;\n      const snapshotUserRole = clientCtx.userRole || 'general';\n      const workingMemoryPresent = atoms.some(a => a.layer === 'working_memory');\n      const semanticMemoryCount = atoms.filter(a => a.layer !== 'working_memory').length;\n      const contextSnapshot =\n        '## CONTEXT SNAPSHOT\\n' +\n        `- Project: ${\n          project_id\n            ? `${snapshotProductName || 'unnamed'} (${snapshotSubmission || 'submission type unknown'})`\n            : 'NOT LOADED'\n        }\\n` +\n        `- Active artifact: ${\n          snapshotArtifactTitle\n            ? `${snapshotArtifactTitle}${snapshotSection ? ` — ${snapshotSection}` : ''}`\n            : 'NONE'\n        }\\n` +\n        `- Memory: working=${workingMemoryPresent ? 'yes' : 'no'}, semantic atoms=${semanticMemoryCount}\\n` +\n        `- Retrieved sources: ${retrievalStatus === 'unavailable' ? 'UNAVAILABLE (the knowledge-base search could not run)' : sources.length}\\n` +\n        `- User role: ${snapshotUserRole}\\n\\n`;\n\n      const systemPrompt =\n        contextSnapshot +\n        intelligencePrefix +\n        projectInstructionsBlock +\n        basePrompt +\n        submissionContextBlock +\n        deviceContextBlock +\n        projectKnowledgeCorpusBlock +\n        sessionBootstrapBlock +\n        memoryBlock +\n        evidenceBlock;\n\n      const gwMessages = [\n        { role: 'system' as const, content: systemPrompt },\n        ...previousMessages\n          .filter((m: any) => m.role === 'user' || m.role === 'assistant')\n          .map((m: any) => ({\n            role: m.role as 'user' | 'assistant',\n            content: m.content,\n          })),\n        { role: 'user' as const, content: message },\n      ];\n\n      console.log(`[AnA] Sending through AI Gateway (${sources.length} sources retrieved)...`);\n      const routingPlan = planKernelExecution({\n        route: '/api/chat',\n        messageLength: message.length,\n        intentLens: orchestratorResult.detectedIntent.lens,\n        intentConfidence: orchestratorResult.detectedIntent.confidence,\n        submissionType: orchestratorResult.detectedSubmissionType,\n        hasEvidence: sources.length > 0,\n        requestedMaxTokens: GENERATION_MAX_TOKENS,\n        requestsGovernedDraft: requestsGovernedDraft(message),\n      });\n      const policyHint = await getKernelPolicyHint({\n        organizationId: numericOrgId ?? null,\n        route: '/api/chat',\n        taskType: routingPlan.taskType,\n      });\n      const selectedStrategy = policyHint?.preferredStrategy || routingPlan.strategy;\n\n      // Validate preferred_provider if provided\n      const VALID_PROVIDERS = ['anthropic', 'openai', 'moonshot'] as const;\n      const validatedChatProvider =\n        preferred_provider && VALID_PROVIDERS.includes(preferred_provider)\n          ? (preferred_provider as (typeof VALID_PROVIDERS)[number])\n          : undefined;\n\n      // Cost-tiered model selection — same policy and precedence as the\n      // ana-ri paths: yields to an explicit provider preference and to a\n      // governance-pinned strategy; opt-out via ANA_MODEL_TIERING=off; tier\n      // remap via ANA_TIER_*_MODEL. No effort picker on this path, so it\n      // resolves at the default Balanced effort. The pinned model rides the\n      // whole agentic loop (the request carries it into every round).\n      const chatTieredModel = (() => {\n        if (validatedChatProvider) return null; // user pinned a provider\n        if (policyHint?.preferredStrategy) return null; // governance owns the strategy\n        if ((process.env.ANA_MODEL_TIERING ?? 'on').toLowerCase() === 'off') return null;\n        const tier = resolveModelTier({\n          effort: 'balanced',\n          riskTier: routingPlan.riskTier,\n          intentLens: orchestratorResult.detectedIntent?.lens,\n          taskType: routingPlan.taskType,\n          substantive: isSubstantiveTurn({\n            messageLength: typeof message === 'string' ? message.length : 0,\n            intentLens: orchestratorResult.detectedIntent?.lens,\n          }),\n        });\n        const enabledModels =\n          typeof gw.getModels === 'function' ? gw.getModels().filter((m) => m.enabled) : [];\n        return resolveTierModel(tier, enabledModels, process.env);\n      })();\n\n      // ── Agentic tool-use loop: AnA can search, check compliance, generate docs ──\n      /* The tenant's permitted tool surface, resolved once for the turn. */\n      const governedTools = await governedToolsetFor(pool, numericOrgId);\n      const baseRequest = {\n        taskType: routingPlan.taskType,\n        // The kernel's risk judgment; see GatewayRequest.riskTier.\n        riskTier: routingPlan.riskTier,\n        messages: gwMessages,\n        temperature: routingPlan.temperature,\n        maxTokens: routingPlan.maxTokens,\n        callerModule: 'ana-ri-chat' as const,\n        organizationId: numericOrgId ?? undefined,\n        userId: numericUserId,\n        strategy: selectedStrategy,\n        // Offer the tools relevant to this turn's intent + context (the platform\n        // command bridge is always included, so nothing is ever truly out of\n        // reach), honouring any tools the user pinned in the tool picker.\n        /* Tenant deny-list FIRST, then relevance. This call assembled the tool\n           surface straight from getAllEnabledTools(), so a tool an organization\n           had switched off in anaToolPolicy.deny was still offered here while\n           the streaming endpoint honoured the setting — a governance control\n           holding on one of two doors. */\n        tools: selectToolsForTurn(governedTools, typeof message === 'string' ? message : '', {\n          pinned: [\n            ...(Array.isArray(selected_tools)\n              ? selected_tools.filter((t: unknown): t is string => typeof t === 'string')\n              : []),\n            /* This route turns navigate_to / act_on_screen / start_product_demo\n               results into offer-chips on the response (collectedNavigation\n               and its siblings below), so the tools have to reach the model\n               whatever the wording scores. They are not always-on in the\n               selector — voice and deep investigations share it and can\n               render no chip — so a caller that can offer a move pins them. */\n            ...SELF_DRIVE_TOOLS,\n          ],\n          context: tool_context && typeof tool_context === 'object' ? tool_context : undefined,\n        }),\n        toolChoice: 'auto' as const,\n        ...(validatedChatProvider ? { provider: validatedChatProvider } : {}),\n        // Mutually exclusive with validatedChatProvider (the tier yields to it).\n        ...(chatTieredModel\n          ? { provider: chatTieredModel.provider, model: chatTieredModel.model }\n          : {}),\n        // A2: cache the (large, stable) in-context corpus prefix when injected.\n        ...(projectKnowledgeCorpusBlock\n          ? { promptCache: { enabled: true, type: 'ephemeral' as const } }\n          : {}),\n      };\n\n      // Use agentic loop for multi-turn tool execution. This generic path has no\n      // effort picker, so it runs at the default (Balanced) round ceiling — up\n      // from the old flat 5 so a deeper investigation can run to completion.\n      loopMessages = baseRequest.messages as Array<{ role: string; content: unknown }>;\n      const gwResponse: AnaGatewayResponse = await executeAgenticLoop(baseRequest, {\n        maxRounds: resolveMaxRounds('balanced'),\n        toolContext: {\n          sqlClient: requestPgClient(req),\n          organizationId: numericOrgId,\n          userId: numericUserId || null,\n          projectId: turnProjectId,\n          // The project as the client sent it: a v2 program's UUID, which the\n          // tools that resolve the open project read (PF-10 S7, as the stream).\n          projectRef: project_id ? String(project_id).replace(/^proj_/, '') : null,\n          // Tenant UUID so the project_knowledge_search tool can scope retrieval.\n          organizationUuid: orgUuid,\n          // Situational context (surface/project/document type) — same signal the\n          // tool selector uses; threaded to handlers for telemetry + tailoring.\n          surface: tool_context && typeof tool_context === 'object' ? ((tool_context as any).surface ?? null) : null,\n          projectType: tool_context && typeof tool_context === 'object' ? ((tool_context as any).projectType ?? null) : null,\n          documentType: tool_context && typeof tool_context === 'object' ? ((tool_context as any).documentType ?? null) : null,\n        },\n        onToolExecution: (toolName, input, result) => {\n          loopCalls.onToolExecution(toolName, input, result);\n          // A navigate_to that resolved against the governed registry becomes\n          // an offer-chip in the response (same contract as the SSE path;\n          // refusals yield null here and never become one).\n          const directive = directiveFromToolResult(toolName, result);\n          if (directive) collectedNavigation.push(directive);\n          // An act_on_screen that resolved against the surface-action registry\n          // becomes an offer-chip the same way (refusals yield null).\n          const actionDirective = surfaceActionFromToolResult(toolName, result);\n          if (actionDirective) collectedSurfaceActions.push(actionDirective);\n          // A demonstration fetched without Live Drive becomes a start chip.\n          const demoStart = demoStartFromToolResult(toolName, result);\n          if (demoStart) collectedDemoStarts.push(demoStart);\n          // A write the partition turned into a proposal: put it to the person.\n          const proposal = pendingSignoffFromToolResult(toolName, result);\n          if (proposal) loopProposals.push(proposal);\n          // Persist the invocation for usage analytics. Latency is 0 here\n          // because the agentic-loop hook fires post-success without a\n          // start timestamp; the streaming path captures real latency.\n          // Errors aren't surfaced through this hook either — see\n          // AnaToolExecutor.executeAgenticLoop's catch branch.\n          void logToolRun({\n            threadId,\n            projectId: turnProjectId,\n            userId: numericUserId || null,\n            organizationId: numericOrgId,\n            toolName,\n            arguments: (input ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>,\n            result: { resultBytes: result.length },\n            status: 'success',\n            latencyMs: 0,\n          });\n        },\n      });\n\n      assistantMessage =\n        gwResponse.content ||\n        'I apologize, but I was unable to generate a response. Please try again.';\n      model = `${gwResponse.provider}/${gwResponse.model}`;\n      provider = gwResponse.provider;\n      servingModel = { provider: gwResponse.provider, model: gwResponse.model, requestId: gwResponse.requestId ?? null };\n      usage = {\n        prompt_tokens: gwResponse.usage.inputTokens,\n        completion_tokens: gwResponse.usage.outputTokens,\n        total_tokens: gwResponse.usage.totalTokens,\n      };\n      latencyMs = gwResponse.latencyMs;\n\n      console.log(\n        `[AnA] AI Gateway response via ${model} (${latencyMs}ms, req=${gwResponse.requestId})`\n      );\n      void logKernelDecision({\n        requestId: gwResponse.requestId,\n        threadId,\n        route: '/api/chat',\n        organizationId: numericOrgId ?? null,\n        userId: numericUserId,\n        projectId: turnProjectId,\n        plannerVersion: routingPlan.plannerVersion,\n        orchestratorName: routingPlan.orchestratorName,\n        intentLens: orchestratorResult.detectedIntent.lens,\n        intentConfidence: orchestratorResult.detectedIntent.confidence,\n        submissionType: orchestratorResult.detectedSubmissionType || null,\n        selectedTaskType: routingPlan.taskType,\n        selectedProvider: gwResponse.provider,\n        selectedModel: gwResponse.model,\n        routingStrategy: selectedStrategy,\n        selectedTools: [],\n        constraints: {\n          ...routingPlan.constraints,\n          maxTokens: routingPlan.maxTokens,\n          temperature: routingPlan.temperature,\n          retrievedSources: sources.length,\n        },\n        decisionRationale: routingPlan.decisionRationale,\n        estimatedCostUsd: gwResponse.usage?.estimatedCostUsd ?? null,\n        latencyMs: gwResponse.latencyMs,\n        outcome: 'success',\n      });\n    } catch (gwError: any) {\n      const budgetRefusal = gwError instanceof ToolResultBudgetExceededError ? gwError : null;\n      const contextRefusal = gwError instanceof GatewayContextWindowError\n        ? classifyGatewayError(gwError, { priorToolWorkMayExist: true }) : null;\n      console.error(budgetRefusal ? '[AnA] Tool result handoff refused:' : '[AnA] AI Gateway call failed:', gwError.message);\n      void logKernelDecision({\n        requestId: `chat-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10)}`,\n        threadId,\n        route: '/api/chat',\n        organizationId: numericOrgId ?? null,\n        userId: numericUserId,\n        projectId: turnProjectId,\n        orchestratorName: 'kernel-router-v1',\n        selectedTaskType: 'chat',\n        routingStrategy: 'quality_optimized',\n        selectedTools: [],\n        decisionRationale: budgetRefusal ? 'AnA tool results exceeded the handoff limit.'\n          : contextRefusal ? 'AnA request exceeded the available context windows.' : 'AnA chat failed during AI Gateway call.',\n        outcome: 'failed',\n        errorMessage: gwError?.message || 'AI Gateway call failed',\n      });\n      const turnRecord = await fileTurn('failed', undefined, gwError);\n      return res.status(budgetRefusal ? 422 : contextRefusal ? GATEWAY_ERROR_HTTP_STATUS[contextRefusal.code] : 503).json({\n        error: budgetRefusal?.message ?? contextRefusal?.message ?? 'AI provider call failed',\n        code: budgetRefusal?.code ?? contextRefusal?.code ?? 'AI_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE',\n        turnRecord,\n      });\n    }\n\n    // ── STEP 6b: AnA's ana-action blocks, as proposals ────────────────\n    // Each block AnA is confident in becomes a create_artifact PROPOSAL through\n    // the command partition — never a write (P0-12 residual, 2026-10-01; until\n    // then this created the artifact, and a review thread in the person's name,\n    // unasked). The proposals are returned as `executedCommands`, the envelope\n    // the sign-off prompt reads (extractPendingSignoffs); a person's yes goes to\n    // POST /api/ana-ri/governed-action, which runs the command.\n    let executedActions: Array<NavigationAction | SurfaceActionChip | DemoStartChip> = [];\n    let actionBlockProposals: CommandResult[] = [];\n\n    if (numericOrgId && numericUserId) {\n      try {\n        // The blocks are the platform's, not the answer: taken out when there\n        // were any, and the answer says what became of each (settleActionBlocks).\n        const settled = await settleActionBlocks(\n          assistantMessage,\n          {\n            // The project resolved once for the turn (PF-10 S10b).\n            projectId: turnProjectId,\n            organizationId: numericOrgId,\n            userId: numericUserId,\n            userName: (req as any).user?.name || (req as any).user?.email || undefined,\n            threadId,\n            servingModel,\n          },\n          loopProposals,\n        );\n        actionBlockProposals = settled.proposals;\n        assistantMessage = settled.answer;\n      } catch (actionErr: any) {\n        // Non-fatal — the answer returns as AnA wrote it, blocks included;\n        // nothing was written.\n        console.warn('[AnA RI] Action-block proposals failed:', actionErr?.message);\n      }\n    }\n    const turnProposals: CommandResult[] = [...loopProposals, ...actionBlockProposals];\n\n    // Navigation chips AFTER guidance actions — same ordering rationale as the\n    // SSE path's post-processing: an artifact the turn actually created still\n    // leads. Deduped + capped by toNavigationActions (first occurrence wins).\n    if (collectedNavigation.length > 0) {\n      executedActions = [...executedActions, ...toNavigationActions(collectedNavigation)];\n    }\n\n    // Surface-action chips under the identical offered-not-performed contract.\n    if (collectedSurfaceActions.length > 0) {\n      executedActions = [...executedActions, ...toSurfaceActionChips(collectedSurfaceActions)];\n    }\n    if (collectedDemoStarts.length > 0) {\n      executedActions = [...executedActions, ...toDemoStartChips(collectedDemoStarts)];\n    }\n\n    // Save to legacy chat_messages for backward compat\n    await saveMessage(threadId, 'user', message, model);\n    await saveMessage(threadId, 'assistant', assistantMessage, model, usage.total_tokens);\n    const turnRecord = await fileTurn('answered', { content: assistantMessage, model, provider });\n\n    // ── STEP 7: PERSIST GENERATION RUN (provenance chain) ──────────────\n    let generationRunId: string | null = null;\n    if (numericOrgId) {\n      try {\n        const answerHash = sha256(assistantMessage);\n        const genResult = await pool.query(\n          `INSERT INTO ai_generation_runs\n             (retrieval_run_id, thread_id, model, provider, answer_hash_sha256,\n              prompt_tokens, completion_tokens, total_tokens, latency_ms, is_demo)\n           VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8, $9, false)\n           RETURNING id`,\n          [\n            retrievalRunId,\n            threadId,\n            model,\n            provider,\n            answerHash,\n            usage.prompt_tokens,\n            usage.completion_tokens,\n            usage.total_tokens,\n            latencyMs,\n          ]\n        );\n        generationRunId = genResult.rows[0].id;\n\n        // Persist assistant ai_message with generation linkage\n        await pool.query(\n          `INSERT INTO ai_messages (thread_id, role, content, generation_run_id)\n           VALUES ($1, 'assistant', $2, $3)`,\n          [threadId, assistantMessage, generationRunId]\n        );\n      } catch (e: any) {\n        if (e?.code !== '42P01') console.warn('[AnA] Generation persist failed:', e.message);\n      }\n    }\n\n    // ── STEP 8: CLAIM SPLIT (paragraph-level) ─────────────────────────\n    const claimTexts = assistantMessage\n      .split(/\\n\\n+/)\n      .map(c => c.trim())\n      .filter(c => c.length > 0);\n\n    interface ClaimResponse {\n      claimId: string | null;\n      claimIndex: number;\n      claimText: string;\n      status: 'SUPPORTED' | 'WEAK' | 'UNSUPPORTED';\n      citations: Array<{ chunkId: string; sourceId: string; title: string; score: number }>;\n      verifierFlags: VerifierFlag[];\n    }\n    const claims: ClaimResponse[] = [];\n\n    // ── STEP 9: CITATION LINKAGE + VERIFIER (per-claim) ───────────────\n    for (let ci = 0; ci < claimTexts.length; ci++) {\n      const claimText = claimTexts[ci];\n      const claimHash = sha256(claimText);\n\n      // Find [SRC-n] refs in this specific claim\n      const claimRefPattern = /\\[SRC-(\\d+)\\]/g;\n      const claimRefs = new Set<number>();\n      let refMatch;\n      while ((refMatch = claimRefPattern.exec(claimText)) !== null) {\n        const idx = parseInt(refMatch[1], 10) - 1;\n        if (idx >= 0 && idx < sources.length) claimRefs.add(idx);\n      }\n\n      // Initial status: SUPPORTED (≥1 citation), UNSUPPORTED (no citations)\n      let status: 'SUPPORTED' | 'WEAK' | 'UNSUPPORTED' =\n        claimRefs.size > 0 ? 'SUPPORTED' : 'UNSUPPORTED';\n\n      let claimId: string | null = null;\n      const citationLinks: ClaimResponse['citations'] = [];\n\n      if (numericOrgId && generationRunId) {\n        try {\n          // Collect citation links first (needed for verifier)\n          for (const refIdx of claimRefs) {\n            const chunk = chunkRows[refIdx];\n            if (chunk) {\n              citationLinks.push({\n                chunkId: chunk.id,\n                sourceId: chunk.atomId,\n                title: sources[refIdx]?.title || '',\n                score: chunk.score,\n              });\n            }\n          }\n\n          // ── STEP 9b: VERIFIER v1 (deterministic) ────────────────────\n          const citScores = citationLinks.map(c => c.score);\n          const citSnippets = Array.from(claimRefs).map(idx => sources[idx]?.content || '');\n          const { flags: verifierFlags, shouldDowngrade } = verifyClaim(\n            claimText,\n            citScores,\n            citSnippets\n          );\n\n          // Downgrade SUPPORTED → WEAK if verifier flags it\n          if (status === 'SUPPORTED' && shouldDowngrade) {\n            status = 'WEAK';\n          }\n\n          // Persist claim with final status + verifier flags\n          const claimResult = await pool.query(\n            `INSERT INTO ai_claims\n               (generation_run_id, claim_index, claim_text, claim_hash_sha256, confidence, status, verifier_flags)\n             VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7) RETURNING id`,\n            [\n              generationRunId,\n              ci,\n              claimText,\n              claimHash,\n              claimRefs.size > 0 ? confidence : null,\n              status,\n              JSON.stringify(verifierFlags),\n            ]\n          );\n          claimId = claimResult.rows[0].id;\n\n          // AnA fix F9: persist all citation linkages in one multi-row INSERT\n          // per claim (replaces a per-citation sequential loop).\n          if (citationLinks.length > 0) {\n            const valuesSql: string[] = [];\n            const params: unknown[] = [];\n            for (let li = 0; li < citationLinks.length; li++) {\n              const o = li * 3;\n              valuesSql.push(`($${o + 1}, $${o + 2}, $${o + 3})`);\n              params.push(claimId, citationLinks[li].chunkId, citationLinks[li].score);\n            }\n            await pool.query(\n              `INSERT INTO ai_claim_citations (claim_id, retrieval_chunk_id, relevance_score)\n               VALUES ${valuesSql.join(', ')}`,\n              params\n            );\n          }\n\n          claims.push({\n            claimId,\n            claimIndex: ci,\n            claimText,\n            status,\n            citations: citationLinks,\n            verifierFlags,\n          });\n          continue; // skip fallback below\n        } catch (e: any) {\n          if (e?.code !== '42P01') console.warn('[AnA] Claim persist failed:', e.message);\n        }\n      }\n\n      // Fallback: no org/generation — still run verifier for response\n      const fallbackScores = citationLinks.map(c => c.score);\n      const fallbackSnippets = Array.from(claimRefs).map(idx => sources[idx]?.content || '');\n      const { flags: fallbackFlags, shouldDowngrade: fallbackDown } = verifyClaim(\n        claimText,\n        fallbackScores,\n        fallbackSnippets\n      );\n      if (status === 'SUPPORTED' && fallbackDown) status = 'WEAK';\n\n      claims.push({\n        claimId,\n        claimIndex: ci,\n        claimText,\n        status,\n        citations: citationLinks,\n        verifierFlags: fallbackFlags,\n      });\n    }\n\n    // ── BUILD BACKWARD-COMPAT CITATIONS MAP
